{"parentUuid":"8a00d3f9-75be-41b8-b84e-adb9289f03a5","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"55ff4cf2-a027-46f4-b24c-a644d6a7248d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"tool_result","content":"Exit code 1\ntitle:\tSpec: Chi Chapter CMS (Delta Tau Delta, Kenyon College)\nstate:\tOPEN\nauthor:\tfaisalsyed922-cloud (Faisal Syed)\nlabels:\tready-for-agent, spec\ncomments:\t0\nassignees:\t\nprojects:\t\nmilestone:\t\nissue-type:\t\nparent:\t\nsub-issues:\tfaisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#2, faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#3, faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#4, faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#5, faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#6, faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#7, faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#8, faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#9, faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#10, faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#11, faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#12, faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#13, faisalsyed922-cloud/iphs400-mp2-cms#14\nsub-issues-completed:\t7/13\nblocked-by:\t\nblocking:\t\nnumber:\t1\n--\n## Problem Statement\n\nThe Chi Chapter of Delta Tau Delta at Kenyon College has an outdated website run by one alum who never updates it. Alumni cannot find updates, information is spread out, and active Officers cannot change anything. Prospective members, alumni, parents and Kenyon staff want to learn about the chapter's members, news, community service and history.\n\nThe chapter president needs a site that the Officers themselves can keep current, with these things never happening:\n\n- A graduated Officer can still log in and post.\n- An Editor deletes or changes the chapter history Page, or another Officer's Post.\n- A half-written Draft goes public, such as a rush announcement with the wrong date.\n- Members' personal information ends up public.\n\nThe people running it are college students who are comfortable with computers but not technical, and their roles change hands about once a year.\n\n## Solution\n\nA small CMS in which the president (Admin) and the other Officers (Editors) write Posts and Pages in a local admin console. The Admin runs `cms publish` to render the Published content into a static site, then `cms deploy` to push it to GitHub Pages after confirming. Nothing is Live until the Admin has reviewed and deployed it.\n\nThe console is deliberately plain. It has a dashboard that leads with what is waiting for release, a content list with a large status badge and only the filters that matter, and an editor with a prominent Preview. An Editor can only change their own Posts and the Pages the Admin has assigned to them. Officers who graduate are Deactivated immediately, and their past work and Byline stay.\n\nThe admin console never goes on the public internet (ADR-001). Editors work on a machine that runs the console, such as a shared chapter-house laptop.\n\n### The 7 required capabilities, for this client\n\n1. **Login with hashed passwords.** An Officer logs in with their email and password. Passwords are stored only as argon2 hashes. A wrong password gets a generic refusal that does not reveal whether the email exists. The Admin sets a temporary password for another User, who must change it at next login. The Admin's own recovery is a command on the machine.\n2. **Two roles, Admin and Editor.** The president is the Admin. Every other Officer is an Editor who writes their own Posts and edits only the Pages the Admin assigned to them. Access is enforced in code on every request, not by hiding links.\n3. **Posts.** Title, Slug, Markdown body, Draft or Published, author, and created, updated and published timestamps. Each Post shows a Byline with the author's full name, title and Term, fixed when the Post is first marked Published.\n4. **Pages in public Navigation.** Standing content such as About, History and Community Service has no author and no date. The Admin creates Pages, assigns one Editor to each, and chooses which appear in Navigation and in what order. One Page is the Home page.\n5. **Admin console.** A dashboard, a filterable content list, and an editor with Markdown Preview.\n6. **Admin-only user management with Deactivation.** The Admin creates Users, sets a role, title and Term, resets passwords, and Deactivates Users when they leave office. Deactivated Users are never deleted and cannot log in, even with an old session.\n7. **Static public site via `cms publish`.** `cms publish` renders only Published content into the static site, with sanitized Markdown and relative paths only. It shows what is new, changed or removed, and refuses if any Draft would appear. `cms deploy` is a separate command that confirms with the Admin before pushing to GitHub Pages.\n\n## User Stories\n\n### Login and accounts\n\n1. As an Officer, I want to log in with my email and password, so that I can reach the console without a separate username.\n2. As an Officer, I want to log out, so that the next person on a shared chapter laptop cannot use my account.\n3. As an Officer who typed a wrong password, I want a clear refusal that does not say whether my email exists, so that nobody can use the login form to find out who has an account.\n4. As an Officer who was just given a temporary password, I want to be made to choose my own at first\n\n... [8087 characters truncated] ...\n\nssword to a temporary one, so that a forgetful Officer can recover without an email server.\n61. As an Editor, I want user management hidden and refused when I try its URLs directly, so that I cannot see or change anyone's account.\n62. As the Admin, I want a setting for the contact line shown to blocked Editors, so that the name and email they are told to contact stay current across turnover.\n\n### Markdown and safety\n\n63. As an Editor, I want basic formatting (headings, bold, italics, lists, links, quotes) to work, so that Posts are readable.\n64. As the Admin, I want all raw HTML in Markdown stripped, so that nobody can embed scripts or markup that is not part of the chapter's design.\n65. As a visitor, I want links that lead off the site to open safely, so that outside pages cannot reach back into the chapter's site.\n66. As a visitor, I want every internal link and file on the public site to use relative paths, so that the site works from the GitHub Pages project URL.\n67. As the Admin, I want all state-changing forms to carry a CSRF token and refuse requests without it, so that another website cannot make my browser change content.\n68. As the Admin, I want Users' emails and login names kept out of the public site, so that members' personal information is never published by the CMS.\n\n### Publishing and Deploy\n\n69. As the Admin, I want `cms publish` to render only Published Posts and Pages into the static site, so that drafts never leave the database.\n70. As the Admin, I want `cms publish` to refuse and explain when any Draft would appear in the output, so that a half-written rush announcement cannot slip through.\n71. As the Admin, I want `cms publish` to show what is new, changed and removed, so that I can review a release before it goes Live.\n72. As the Admin, I want `cms publish` to render Navigation, Home, News and each Post's Byline, so that the generated site is complete and I only run one command.\n73. As the Admin, I want the News page to list Posts newest-first by published date, so that alumni see the latest chapter news first.\n74. As the Admin, I want Home to show the Home Page followed by the 5 latest Posts, so that visitors see current activity straight away.\n75. As the Admin, I want `cms deploy` to ask me to confirm before it pushes, so that I give final approval to everything that becomes Live.\n76. As the Admin, I want `cms deploy` to push only the rendered static site, so that the database, `.env` and secrets are never published.\n77. As the Admin, I want unpublished (set back to Draft) content to disappear from the site at the next release, so that taking something down is possible.\n78. As a visitor (prospective member, alumni, parent or Kenyon staff), I want to read the chapter's news, community service and history without logging in, so that I can learn about the chapter.\n.claude/hooks/ctx_compact_log.py\n.claude/hooks/ctx_guard.py\n.claude/hooks/ctx_statusline.py\n.claude/hooks/save_transcript.py\n.claude/settings.json\n.env.example\n.gitignore\n2026-09-29-142302-explore-this-macbook-pro-m4-sequoia-macos-system.txt\n2026-10-04-031735-local-command-caveatcaveat-the-messages-below.txt\nCLAUDE.md\nCONTEXT.md\nREADME.md\nSECURITY-CHECKLIST.md\napp/__init__.py\napp/authz.py\napp/cli.py\napp/deploy.py\napp/main.py\napp/markdown.py\napp/pages.py\napp/posts.py\napp/publish.py\napp/routes/__init__.py\napp/routes/auth.py\napp/routes/pages.py\napp/routes/posts.py\napp/routes/user_password.py\napp/routes/users.py\napp/security.py\napp/settings.py\napp/templating.py\napp/users.py\ndev/manual_setup_20260922.md\ndocs/adr/ADR-001-local-admin-static-public.md\ndocs/adr/ADR-002-stack.md\ndocs/agents/domain.md\ndocs/agents/issue-tracker.md\ndocs/agents/triage-labels.md\ndocs/handoff/.gitkeep\ndocs/manual_iphs400_mp2-web-cms_20260922.md\ndocs/mp2-grading-rubric_20260922.md\ndocs/mp2-setup_context-threshold-hook_20260922.md\ndocs/process/.gitkeep\ndocs/process/compaction-log.md\ndocs/screenshots/.gitkeep\nnotes/.gitkeep\nnotes/client-brief.md\nnotes/cms-field-notes.md\nnotes/report-scratch.md\nnotes/setup_check.md\nnotes/token-budget-plan.md\nnotes/usage-ledger.csv\npyproject.toml\nscripts/check_submission.py\nscripts/seed_demo.py\nscripts/usage_report.py\nskills-lock.json\ntemplates/admin/change_password.html\ntemplates/admin/forbidden.html\ntemplates/admin/hello.html\ntemplates/admin/login.html\ntemplates/admin/page_form.html\ntemplates/admin/pages.html\ntemplates/admin/post_form.html\ntemplates/admin/posts.html\ntemplates/admin/preview.html\ntemplates/admin/user_edit.html\ntemplates/admin/user_new.html\ntemplates/admin/user_temp_password.html\ntemplates/admin/users.html\ntemplates/base.html\ntemplates/public/home.html\ntemplates/public/news.html\ntemplates/public/page.html\ntemplates/public/post.html\ntests/conftest.py\ntests/test_accounts_ That is the whole skeleton — it exists so you can prove the stack\nruns before you build anything on it.\n\nAdd your routes in their own modules (app/routes/posts.py and so on) and include\nthem here. Keep this file small.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import pages, posts, settings, users\nfrom app.routes import auth, pages as pages_routes, posts as posts_routes, user_password, users as users_routes\nfrom app.security import SESSION_SECONDS, SkewTolerantSessionMiddleware, AdminRequired, LoginRequired, PasswordChangeRequired, csrf_token, current_user\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\n\ndef create_app(database: Path | None = None) -> FastAPI:\n    app = FastAPI(title=\"Chi Chapter CMS\")\n    app.state.database = Path(database or settings.DATABASE_PATH)\n    users.init_db(app.state.database)\n    posts.init_posts(app.state.database)\n    pages.init_pages(app.state.database)\n    app.add_middleware(SkewTolerantSessionMiddleware, secret_key=settings.SECRET_KEY,\n                       max_age=SESSION_SECONDS, same_site=\"lax\")\n\n    @app.exception_handler(LoginRequired)\n    def send_to_login(request: Request, exc: LoginRequired):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/login\", status_code=303)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(PasswordChangeRequired)\n    def must_change(request: Request, exc: PasswordChangeRequired):\n        return RedirectResponse(\"/user/password\", status_code=303)\n\n    @app.exception_handler(AdminRequired)\n    def admin_only(request: Request, exc: AdminRequired):\n        db = request.app.state.database\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/forbidden.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"Admin only\", \"admin\": users.current_admin(db),\n             \"contact_line\": users.get_setting(db, \"contact_line\")},\n            status_code=403)\n\n    app.include_router(auth.router)\n    app.include_router(user_password.router)\n    app.include_router(users_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(posts_routes.router)\n    app.include_router(pages_routes.router)\n\n    @app.get(\"/admin\")\n    def admin_home(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"admin/hello.html\",\n            {\"title\": \"Admin\", \"user\": user, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request)}\n        )\n\n    @app.get(\"/\")\n    def public_home(request: Request):\n        return templates.TemplateResponse(\n            request, \"public/home.html\",\n            {\"title\": settings.SITE_TITLE, \"subtitle\": settings.SITE_SUBTITLE,\n             \"items\": []},\n        )\n\n    # Your ticket work plugs in here, e.g.\n    #   from app.routes import posts\n    #   app.include_router(posts.router)\n    return app\n\n\napp = create_app()\n\"\"\"Posts: list, create, edit, publish, preview, delete.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import authz, posts, users\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.publish import navigation\nfrom app.security import csrf_token, current_user, verify_csrf\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/posts\")\nguarded = [Depends(current_user), Depends(verify_csrf)]\n\nACTIONS = {\"save\", \"publish\", \"draft\"}\n\n\ndef load_post(request: Request, user, post_id: int, allowed) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Fetch a Post the current User is allowed to act on, or 404/403.\"\"\"\n    post = posts.get_post(request.app.state.database, post_id)\n    if post is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"No such post.\")\n    if not allowed(user, post):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"You can only change your own Posts.\")\n    return post\n\n\ndef render_form(request: Request, user, *, post=None, form=None, error=None,\n                status_code=200):\n    if form is None:\n        form = ({\"title\": post[\"title\"], \"slug\": post[\"slug\"], \"body\": post[\"body\"]}\n                if post else {})\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/post_form.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit post\" if post else \"New post\", \"user\": user, \"post\": post,\n         \"form\": form, \"error\": error, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n         \"byline\": posts.byline(post) if post and post[\"published_at\"] else None,\n         \"can_edit_slug\": authz.can_edit_slug(user, post) if post else True,\n         \"can_unlock_slug\": bool(post and post[\"slug_locked\"]\n                                 and authz.can_unlock_slug(user)),\n         \"can_delete\": bool(post and authz.can_delete_post(user, post))},\n        status_code=status_code)\n\n\n@router.get(\"\")\ndef list_view(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/posts.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Posts\", \"user\": user, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n         \"posts\": posts.list_posts(request.app.state.database),\n         \"can_edit\": authz.can_edit_post})\n\n\n@router.get(\"/new\")\ndef new_form(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    return render_form(request, user)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/preview\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef preview(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n            body: str = Form(\"\"), post_id: int | None = Form(None)):\n    author = user\n    byline = None\n    if post_id is not None:\n        post = load_post(request, user, post_id, authz.can_edit_post)\n        author = users.get_user(request.app.state.database, post[\"author_id\"])\n        byline = posts.byline(post) if post[\"published_at\"] else None\n    if byline is None:\n        byline = posts.format_byline(author[\"display_name\"], author[\"title\"],\n                                     author[\"term\"])\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/preview.html\",\n        {\"title\": title or \"Untitled\", \"html\": render_markdown(body),\n         \"byline\": byline, \"nav_links\": navigation(request.app.state.database),\n         \"nav_preview\": True})\n\n\n@router.post(\"\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef create(request: Request, user=Depends(current_user), title: str = Form(\"\"),\n           slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"), action: str = Form(\"save\")):\n    form = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}\n    if not title.strip():\n        return render_form(request, user, form=form, error=\"A Post needs a title.\",\n                           status_code=400)\n    try:\n        post_id = posts.create_post(\n            request.app.state.database, author=user, title=title.strip(),\n            slug=posts.slugify(slug) if slug.strip() else \"\", body=body,\n            publish=action == \"publish\")\n    except posts.DuplicateSlug:\n        return render_form(request, user, form=form, status_code=400,\n                           error=\"That Slug is already used by another Post.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.get(\"/{post_id:int}\")\ndef edit_form(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    post = load_post(request, user, post_id, authz.can_edit_post)\n    return render_form(request, user, post=post)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id:int}\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef save(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user),\n         title: str = Form(\"\"), slug: str = Form(\"\"), body: str = Form(\"\"),\n         action: str = Form(\"save\")):\n    post = load_post(request, user, post_id, authz.can_edit_post)\n    if action not in ACTIONS:\n        raise HTTPException(400, \"Unknown action.\")\n    form = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body}\n    if not title.strip():\n        return render_form(request, user, post=post, form=form, status_code=400,\n                           error=\"A Post needs a title.\")\n    new_slug = None\n    if authz.can_edit_slug(user, post):\n        new_slug = posts.slugify(slug) if slug.strip() else post[\"slug\"]\n    try:\n        posts.update_post(request.app.state.database, post_id, title=title.strip(),\n                          body=body, slug=new_slug, action=action)\n    except posts.DuplicateSlug:\n        return render_form(request, user, post=post, form=form, status_code=400,\n                           error=\"That Slug is already used by another Post.\")\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id:int}/unlock-slug\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef unlock_slug(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    if not authz.can_unlock_slug(user):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"Only the Admin can unlock a Slug.\")\n    if posts.get_post(request.app.state.database, post_id) is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"No such post.\")\n    posts.unlock_slug(request.app.state.database, post_id)\n    return RedirectResponse(f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\", status_code=303)\n\n\n@router.post(\"/{post_id:int}/delete\", dependencies=guarded)\ndef delete(request: Request, post_id: int, user=Depends(current_user)):\n    load_post(request, user, post_id, authz.can_delete_post)\n    posts.delete_post(request.app.state.database, post_id)\n    return RedirectResponse(\"/admin/posts\", status_code=303)\n\"\"\"Pages: list, create, edit, publish, preview, delete. Navigation and the Home\nflag are Admin-only; an Editor works only on the Page assigned to them.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Form, HTTPException, Request\nfrom fastapi.responses import RedirectResponse\n\nfrom app import authz, pages, posts\nfrom app.markdown import render_markdown\nfrom app.publish import navigation\nfrom app.security import csrf_token, current_user, require_admin, verify_csrf\nfrom app.templating import templates\n\nrouter = APIRouter(prefix=\"/admin/pages\")\nguarded = [Depends(current_user), Depends(verify_csrf)]\nadmin_guarded = [Depends(require_admin), Depends(verify_csrf)]\n\nACTIONS = {\"save\", \"publish\", \"draft\"}\nTAKEN = \"That Slug is already used by another Page.\"\n\n\ndef load_page(request: Request, user, page_id: int, allowed=authz.can_edit_page):\n    page = pages.get_page(request.app.state.database, page_id)\n    if page is None:\n        raise HTTPException(404, \"No such page.\")\n    if not allowed(user, page):\n        raise HTTPException(403, \"You can only change the Page assigned to you.\")\n    return page\n\n\ndef render_form(request: Request, user, *, page=None, form=None, error=None,\n                status_code=200):\n    db = request.app.state.database\n    if form is None:\n        form = ({\"title\": page[\"title\"], \"slug\": page[\"slug\"], \"body\": page[\"body\"],\n                 \"assigned_editor_id\": page[\"assigned_editor_id\"],\n                 \"show_in_nav\": page[\"show_in_nav\"], \"nav_order\": page[\"nav_order\"],\n                 \"is_home\": page[\"is_home\"]} if page else {\"nav_order\": 0})\n    return templates.TemplateResponse(\n        request, \"admin/page_form.html\",\n        {\"title\": \"Edit page\" if page else \"New page\", \"user\": user, \"page\": page,\n         \"form\": form, \"error\": error, \"csrf_token\": csrf_token(request),\n         \"is_admin\": authz.is_admin(user), \"editors\": pages.active_editors(db),\n         \"can_edit_slug\": authz.can_edit_slug(user, page) if page else True,\n         \"can_unlock_slug\": bool(page and body text not null default '',\n    status text not null default 'draft' check (status in ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id integer not null references users(id),\n    created_at text not null,\n    updated_at text not null,\n    published_at text,\n    slug_locked integer not null default 0,\n    byline_name text,\n    byline_title text,\n    byline_term text\n)\n\"\"\"\n\n\nclass DuplicateSlug(Exception):\n    pass\n\n\ndef now() -> str:\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(timespec=\"seconds\")\n\n\ndef slugify(title: str) -> str:\n    text = None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(_SCHEMA)\n\n\ndef get_post(database: Path, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select posts.*, users.display_name as author_name from posts\"\n            \" join users on users.id = posts.author_id where posts.id = ?\",\n            (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_posts(database: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select posts.*, users.display_name as author_name from posts\"\n            \" join users on users.id = posts.author_id\"\n            \" order by posts.updated_at desc, posts.id desc\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef create_post(database: Path, *, author, title: str, slug: str, body: str,\n                publish: bool = False) -> int:\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            cur = conn.execute(\n                \"insert into posts (title, slug, body, author_id, created_at,\"\n                \" updated_at) values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug or slugify(title), body, author[\"id\"], stamp, stamp))\n            post_id = cur.lastrowid\n            if publish:\n                _publish(conn, post_id, author, stamp)\n            return post_id\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        if \"posts.slug\" in str(exc):\n            raise DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef update_post(database: Path, post_id: int, *, title: str, body: str,\n                slug: str | None, action: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Save changes. `slug` is None when the caller may not change it.\"\"\"\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            if slug is None:\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set title=?, body=?, updated_at=?\"\n                             \" where id=?\", (title, body, stamp, post_id))\n            else:\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set title=?, body=?, slug=?,\"\n                             \" updated_at=? where id=?\",\n                             (title, body, slug or slugify(title), stamp, post_id))\n            if action == \"publish\":\n                author = conn.execute(\n                    \"select users.* from users join posts\"\n                    \" on posts.author_id = users.id where posts.id=?\",\n                    (post_id,)).fetchone()\n                _publish(conn, post_id, author, stamp)\n            elif action == in str(exc):\n            raise DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef _publish(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, author, stamp: str) -> None:\n    first = conn.execute(\"select published_at from posts where id=?\",\n                         (post_id,)).fetchone()[\"published_at\"] is None\n    conn.execute(\"update posts set status='published' where id=?\", (post_id,))\n    if first:  # published timestamp, Byline and Slug lock are fixed at first publish\n        conn.execute(\n            \"update posts set published_at=?, slug_locked=1, byline_name=?,\"\n            \" byline_title=?, byline_term=? where id=?\",\n            (stamp, author[\"display_name\"], author[\"title\"], author[\"term\"],\n             post_id))\n\n\ndef unlock_slug(database: Path, post_id: with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"delete from posts where id=?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef format_byline(name, title, term) -> str:\n    return \", \".join(p for p in (name, title, int) -> bool:\n    return any(u[\"id\"] == user_id for u in active_editors(database))\n\n\ndef _set_home(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\"update pages set is_home = (id = ?)\", (page_id,))\n\n\ndef create_page(database: Path, *, title: str, slug: str, body: str,\n                assigned_editor_id: int | None, show_in_nav: bool, nav_order: int,\n                is_home: bool, publish: bool = False) -> int:\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            first = conn.execute(\"select count(*) from pages\").fetchone()[0] == 0\n            cur = conn.execute(\n                \"insert into pages (title, slug, body, assigned_editor_id,\"\n                \" show_in_nav, nav_order, created_at, updated_at)\"\n                \" values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug or slugify(title), body, assigned_editor_id,\n                 int(show_in_nav), nav_order, stamp, stamp))\n            page_id = cur.lastrowid\n            if is_home or first:  # there is always exactly one Home page\n                _set_home(conn, page_id)\n            if publish:\n                _publish(conn, page_id, update_page(database: Path, page_id: int, *, title: str, body: str,\n                slug: str | None, action: str, admin_fields: dict | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Save changes. `slug` is None when the caller may not change it, and\n    `admin_fields` (assigned_editor_id, show_in_nav, nav_order, is_home) is None\n    when the caller is not the Admin.\"\"\"\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n slug is not None:\n                conn.execute(\"update pages set slug=? where id=?\",\n                             (slug or slugify(title), page_id))\n            if admin_fields is not None:\n (admin_fields[\"assigned_editor_id\"],\n                     int(admin_fields[\"show_in_nav\"]), admin_fields[\"nav_order\"],\n                     page_id))\n                if admin_fields[\"is_home\"]:\n                    _set_home(conn, page_id)\n            if action == \"publish\":\n                _publish(conn, page_id, stamp)\n            elif action == \"draft\":\n DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef _publish(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int, stamp: str) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\"update pages set status='published' where id=?\", (page_id,))\n    conn.execute(\"update pages set published_at=?, slug_locked=1 where id=?\"\n                 \" and published_at is null\", (stamp, page_id))\n\n\ndef unlock_slug(database: Path, page_id: int) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"update pages set slug_locked=0 where id=?\", (page_id,))\n\n\ndef delete_page(database: Path, page_id: int) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        page = conn.execute(\"select is_home from pages where id=?\",\n                            (page_id,)).fetchone()\n        others = conn.execute(\"select count(*) from pages where id != ?\",\n                              (page_id,)).fetchone()[0]\n        if page and page[\"is_home\"] and others:\n            raise HomePage\n        conn.execute(\"delete from None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(_SCHEMA)\n\n\ndef get_post(database: Path, post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select posts.*, users.display_name as author_name from posts\"\n            \" join users on users.id = posts.author_id where posts.id = ?\",\n            (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_posts(database: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\n            \"select posts.*, users.display_name as author_name from posts\"\n            \" join users on users.id = posts.author_id\"\n            \" order by posts.updated_at desc, posts.id desc\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef create_post(database: Path, *, author, title: str, slug: str, body: str,\n                publish: bool = False) -> int:\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            cur = conn.execute(\n                \"insert into posts (title, slug, body, author_id, created_at,\"\n                \" updated_at) values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n                (title, slug or slugify(title), body, author[\"id\"], stamp, stamp))\n            post_id = cur.lastrowid\n            if publish:\n                _publish(conn, post_id, author, stamp)\n            return post_id\n    except sqlite3.IntegrityError as exc:\n        if \"posts.slug\" in str(exc):\n            raise DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef update_post(database: Path, post_id: int, *, title: str, body: str,\n                slug: str | None, action: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Save changes. `slug` is None when the caller may not change it.\"\"\"\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            if slug is None:\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set title=?, body=?, updated_at=?\"\n                             \" where id=?\", (title, body, stamp, post_id))\n            else:\n                conn.execute(\"update posts set title=?, body=?, slug=?,\"\n                             \" updated_at=? where id=?\",\n                             (title, body, slug or slugify(title), stamp, post_id))\n            if action == \"publish\":\n                author = conn.execute(\n                    \"select users.* from users join posts\"\n in str(exc):\n            raise DuplicateSlug from exc\n        raise\n\n\ndef _publish(conn: sqlite3.Connection, post_id: int, author, stamp: str) -> None:\n    first = conn.execute(\"select published_at from posts where id=?\",\n                         (post_id,)).fetchone()[\"published_at\"] is None\n    conn.execute(\"update posts set status='published' where id=?\", (post_id,))\n    if first:  # published timestamp, Byline and Slug lock are fixed at first publish\n        conn.execute(\n            \"update posts set published_at=?, slug_locked=1, byline_name=?,\"\n            \" byline_title=?, byline_term=? where id=?\",\n            (stamp, author[\"display_name\"], author[\"title\"], author[\"term\"],\n             post_id))\n\n\ndef unlock_slug(database: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"update posts set slug_locked=0 where id=?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef delete_post(database: Path, post_id: int) -> None:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        conn.execute(\"delete from posts where id=?\", (post_id,))\n\n\ndef format_byline(name, title, term) -> str:\n    return \", \".join(p for p in (name, title, term) if p)\n\n\ndef byline(post) -> str:\n    return format_byline(post[\"byline_name\"], post[\"byline_title\"],\n                         post[\"byline_term\"])\n\"\"\"Page storage. A Page is standing content: no author, no date on the public site.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport sqlite3\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom app.posts import now, slugify\nfrom app.users import connect\n\n_SCHEMA = \"\"\"\ncreate table if not exists pages (\n    id integer primary key,\n    title text not null,\n    slug text not null unique,\n    body text not null default default 0\n)\n\"\"\"\n\n_SELECT = (\"select pages.*, users.display_name as editor_name from pages\"\n           \" left join users on users.id = pages.assigned_editor_id\")\n\n\nclass DuplicateSlug(Exception):\n    pass\n\n\nclass HomePage(Exception):\n    \"\"\"The Home page cannot be deleted while other Pages exist.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef init_pages(database: Path) -> None:\n    with conn.execute(_SELECT + \" where pages.id = ?\", (page_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef list_pages(database: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(_SELECT + \" order by pages.title\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef count_unassigned(database: Path) -> int:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"select count(*) from pages\"\n                            \" where assigned_editor_id is null\").fetchone()[0]\n\n\ndef active_editors(database: Path) -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect(database) as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"select * from users where role = 'editor'\"\n                            \" and is_active = 1 order by display_name\").fetchall()\n\n\ndef is_assignable(database: Path, user_id: int) -> bool:\n    return any(u[\"id\"] == user_id for u in active_editors(database))\n\n\ndef _set_home(conn: sqlite3.Connection, page_id: int) -> None:\n    conn.execute(\"update pages set is_home = (id = ?)\", (page_id,))\n\n\ndef create_page(database: Path, *, title: str, slug: str, body: str,\n                assigned_editor_id: int | None, show_in_nav: bool, nav_order: int,\n                is_home: bool, publish: bool = False) -> int:\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n        with connect(database) as conn:\n            first = conn.execute(\"select count(*) from pages\").fetchone()[0] == 0\n            cur = conn.execute(\n                \"insert into pages (title, slug, body, assigned_editor_id,\"\n                \" show_in_nav, nav_order, created_at, updated_at)\"\n cur.lastrowid\n            if is_home or first:  # there is always exactly one Home page\n                _set_home(conn, page_id)\n            if publish:\n                _publish(conn, page_id, update_page(database: Path, page_id: int, *, title: str, body: str,\n                slug: str | None, action: str, admin_fields: dict | None = None) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Save changes. `slug` is None when the caller may not change it, and\n    `admin_fields` (assigned_editor_id, show_in_nav, nav_order, is_home) is None\n    when the caller is not the Admin.\"\"\"\n    stamp = now()\n    try:\n (admin_fields[\"assigned_editor_id\"],\n                     int(admin_fields[\"show_in_nav\"]), admin_fields[\"nav_order\"],\n                     page_id))\n                if    conn.execute(\"update pages set published_at=?, slug_locked=1 where id=?\"\n                 \" and published_at is null\", (stamp, page_id))\n\n\ndef unlock_slug(database: Path, page_id: int) -> {"parentUuid":"dc9bbaaf-752a-4aea-acea-549531a2f4bf","isSidechain":false,"attachment":{"type":"hook_success","hookName":"PreToolUse:Bash","toolUseID":"toolu_01LDTQ9mwwfS5jCQF2r1vwTb","hookEvent":"PreToolUse","content":"","stdout":"{\"hookSpecificOutput\":{\"hookEventName\":\"PreToolUse\",\"permissionDecisionReason\":\"RTK auto-rewrite\",\"updatedInput\":{\"command\":\"gh issue view 1 --json body -q .body | grep -n -i -E \\\"dashboard|list|badge|pending|mine|filter|deploy\\\" | head -40; sed -n 80,140p tests/conftest.py; sed -n 1,50p tests/test_pages.py; rtk grep -n \\\"deploy\\\" app/cli.py | head; cat app/security.py | sed -n 1,96p; cat .claude/skills/tdd/SKILL.md 2>/dev/null | head -5; rtk read templates/admin/pages.html\"}}}\n","stderr":"","exitCode":0,"command":"rtk hook claude","durationMs":64},"type":"attachment","uuid":"32385db9-d28e-4e87-8899-865adcbe667d","timestamp":"2026-10-06T23:50:56.623Z","session_id":"4b1a37e3-ea11-48e9-baa9-b66b752b7a0d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/home/faisa/code/iphs400-mp2-cms","sessionId":"a1a5bdf6-4bfd-4eda-8bad-3550c58ce43e","version":"2.1.292","gitBranch":"main"}
{"parentUuid":"dc9bbaaf-752a-4aea-acea-549531a2f4bf","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"55ff4cf2-a027-46f4-b24c-a644d6a7248d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01LDTQ9mwwfS5jCQF2r1vwTb","type":"tool_result","content":"16:A small CMS in which the president (Admin) and the other Officers (Editors) write Posts and Pages in a local admin console. The Admin runs `cms publish` to render the Published content into a static site, then `cms deploy` to push it to GitHub Pages after confirming. Nothing is Live until the Admin has reviewed and deployed it.\n18:The console is deliberately plain. It has a dashboard that leads with what is waiting for release, a content list with a large status badge and only the filters that matter, and an editor with a prominent Preview. An Editor can only change their own Posts and the Pages the Admin has assigned to them. Officers who graduate are Deactivated immediately, and their past work and Byline stay.\n28:5. **Admin console.** A dashboard, a filterable content list, and an editor with Markdown Preview.\n30:7. **Static public site via `cms publish`.** `cms publish` renders only Published content into the static site, with sanitized Markdown and relative paths only. It shows what is new, changed or removed, and refuses if any Draft would appear. `cms deploy` is a separate command that confirms with the Admin before pushing to GitHub Pages.\n50:12. As an Editor, I want to see every Post and Page in the content list but have other people's rows read-only, so that I know what is already written without being able to change it.\n73:32. As an Editor, I want Posts that changed since the last Deploy to be marked in the content list, so that I can see which edits are not yet Live.\n80:36. As a visitor, I want a Published Page without a Navigation entry to still be reachable by its URL, so that unlisted pages such as a rush information page can be shared directly.\n88:41. As an Officer, I want a dashboard that leads with Pending release, so that I see first what is waiting to go public.\n89:42. As an Officer, I want the dashboard to show Drafts waiting and my own recent items, so that I can pick up where I left off.\n90:43. As the Admin, I want the dashboard to show Pages needing a new Assigned editor, so that turnover problems are visible.\n91:44. As an Officer, I want a content list with a large, clearly coloured status badge for Draft and Published, so that I can tell the status at a glance.\n92:45. As an Officer, I want to filter the content list by status, by type (Post or Page) and by author, so that I can find things quickly.\n93:46. As an Officer, I want a \"Mine\" shortcut on the content list, so that I can see only my own work in one click.\n94:47. As an Officer, I want the content list to have no categories, tags or bulk actions, so that it makes sense without a technical background.\n109:59. As the Admin, I want the user list to warn me when Pages lose their Assigned editor, so that I reassign them during turnover.\n116:63. As an Editor, I want basic formatting (headings, bold, italics, lists, links, quotes) to work, so that Posts are readable.\n123:### Publishing and Deploy\n129:73. As the Admin, I want the News page to list Posts newest-first by published date, so that alumni see the latest chapter news first.\n131:75. As the Admin, I want `cms deploy` to ask me to confirm before it pushes, so that I give final approval to everything that becomes Live.\n132:76. As the Admin, I want `cms deploy` to push only the rendered static site, so that the database, `.env` and secrets are never published.\n139:80. As the outgoing president, I want a handover checklist in the README, so that my successor knows what to take over (the repository, the database and the image folder) and what to do first.\n149:87. As the Admin, I want `cms deploy` to warn me and stop if content has changed since the last `cms publish`, so that stale files in the static site are not pushed by accident.\n150:88. As the Admin, I want the `cms publish` summary to compare against what was last Deployed, so that \"changed\" means changed since the public site was updated.\n154:**Architecture (fixed by ADR-001 and ADR-002):** a FastAPI admin console with Jinja templates and SQLite runs locally. `cms publish` writes a static site, and `cms deploy` pushes it to the `gh-pages` branch. There is no frontend framework and no build step. This spec adds no dependency. Asking the user is required before adding one.\n168:- **Admin console:** dashboard (Pending release first, then Drafts waiting, then my recent items, and for the Admin only Pages needing an editor), content list (status, type and author filters, \"Mine\" shortcut, large status badge, read-only rows for other people's items), editor with Preview (opens in a new tab with a \"draft preview, not public\" banner, refreshed on click), user management, and the blocked-Editor message that names the Admin and the contact line.\n170:- **Publishing:** reads only Published content, renders pages, Posts, Home, News and Navigation into the static site using relative paths only, then verifies that no Draft appears in the output. Home is the Home Page followed by the 5 latest Posts as title, date and first paragraph. News lists every Post newest-first, with titles and dates, on one page.\n171:- **CLI:** `cms publish` renders, shows new, changed and removed items, and refuses on any Draft in the output. `cms deploy` is separate, asks for confirmation, then pushes. `cms create-admin <email>` takes a hidden password prompt. `cms reset-password <email>` resets the Admin's own password on the machine. Both take an email, not a username, to match login.\n181:- **Deploy fingerprints:** a successful `cms deploy` records a fingerprint of each item, the baseline for \"changed since last Deploy\" in the `cms publish` summary and for the stale-site warning in `cms deploy`. Without this extra, the summary lists every Published item.\n188:1. **The HTTP app through the test client** (`client` and the logged-in `client_as(\"admin\" | \"editor\")`). This covers login and logout, roles and permission refusals (including direct URL requests), Posts and Pages (Slug rules, the Draft and Published rules, Assigned editor, Byline snapshot), the dashboard, content list and filters, Preview, user management and Deactivation, and CSRF (replaying each POST with the token removed). Assertions are on status codes, redirects and response HTML.\n189:2. **Static output and the `cms` commands, against a temporary site folder and a temporary database.** `render_site` covers the content of the output: only Published content, no root-absolute paths, `<script>` and `onerror=` removed, Navigation order, Home and News, bylines with no email or login name. The `cms` command entry point covers `publish` refusing when a Draft would appear and `deploy` asking before pushing, with the actual push stubbed so nothing touches `gh-pages`.\n193:**Security checklist coverage:** each of the 10 items in the project's security checklist gets at least one test through the seams above, including the editor-cannot-reach-admin-routes and anonymous-is-redirected tests.\n198:- Remote access for Editors. The console stays local (ADR-001), and live admin deployment is a stretch goal.\n200:- Categories, tags and bulk actions in the content list.\n202:- A public member roster page or any automated listing of members.\n207:- Any change to the stack in the project's dependency list.\n211:- **Build order:** the 7 required capabilities come first. The five cuttable extras (images, event date, `cms backup`, login lockout, deploy fingerprints) are the last tickets so they can be cut if time runs out. The core must work without them.\n212:- **Vocabulary:** use the terms in `CONTEXT.md`: Officer, User, Admin, Editor, Deactivated, Term, Byline, Post, Page, Assigned editor, Draft, Published, Live, Publish run, Deploy, Pending release, Navigation and Slug. \"Published\" means ready for release and \"Live\" means actually on the public site as of the latest Deploy.\n        # proves the session was started.\n        assert response.status_code in (302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}: expected a redirect\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n\"\"\"T07: Pages, Assigned editor and Navigation, tested through the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nimport pytest\nfrom conftest import token_from\n\nfrom app import users\n\nEDITOR_ID = 2\n\n\ndef csrf(c):\n    return token_from(c.get(\"/admin\").text)\n\n\ndef save_page(c, title=\"About\", slug=\"\", body=\"About us.\", action=\"save\",\n              page_id=None, **extra):\n    path = f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\" if page_id else \"/admin/pages\"\n    data = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body, \"action\": action,\n            \"csrf_token\": csrf(c), **extra}\n    return c.post(path, data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef page_id_of(response):\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/admin/pages/(\\d+)\", response.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef page_post(c, page_id, suffix, **data):\n    return c.post(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}{suffix}\",\n                  data={\"csrf_token\": csrf(c), **data}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef row(db_path, page_id, column):\n    return sqlite3.connect(db_path).execute(\n        f\"select {column} from pages where id=?\", (page_id,)).fetchone()[0]\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef other_editor(db_path, make_client):\n    users.create_user(db_path, email=\"other@example.test\", password=\"other-editor-pw\",\n                      role=\"editor\", display_name=\"Other Editor\")\n    c = make_client()\n    c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"other@example.test\", \"password\": \"other-editor-pw\",\n                           \"csrf_token\": token_from(c.get(\"/login\").text)})\n    return c\n\n\n# --- creating and permissions ----------------------------------------------\n1:\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n5:    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n15:from app import deploy, settings, users\n19:def main(argv: list[str] | None = None, *, push=deploy.push_site,\n28:    deploy_cmd = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n29:    deploy_cmd.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n99:    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n103:        bad = deploy.root_absolute_files(settings.SITE)\n105:            print(\"Refusing to deploy: root-absolute paths (href=\\\"/...\\\") in \"\n110:            print(\"Not deployed. Nothing was pushed.\")\n\"\"\"Sessions, CSRF protection and the login requirement for admin routes.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\nfrom itsdangerous import SignatureExpired, TimestampSigner\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import users\n\nSESSION_SECONDS = 8 * 60 * 60\n# How far in the future a validly signed session may look. A wall clock that\n# steps backwards (WSL2 and VM clock syncs do) would otherwise log everyone out.\nCLOCK_SKEW_SECONDS = 5 * 60\n\n\nclass SkewTolerantSigner(TimestampSigner):\n    \"\"\"itsdangerous rejects a cookie stamped even 1s ahead of the clock; allow a little.\"\"\"\n\n    def unsign(self, signed_value, max_age=None, return_timestamp=False):\n        try:\n            return super().unsign(signed_value, max_age=max_age,\n                                  return_timestamp=return_timestamp)\n        except SignatureExpired as exc:\n            signed = exc.date_signed\n            # Only raised after the signature itself verified.\n            ahead = signed.timestamp() - self.get_timestamp() if signed else 0\n            if not 0 < ahead <= CLOCK_SKEW_SECONDS:\n                raise\n            value = exc.payload\n            return (value, signed) if return_timestamp else value\n\n\nclass SkewTolerantSessionMiddleware(SessionMiddleware):\n    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):\n        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)\n        self.signer = SkewTolerantSigner(str(kwargs.get(\"secret_key\") or args[1]))\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised for an anonymous (or Deactivated) visitor; becomes a redirect.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every state-changing route: reject a missing/wrong token.\"\"\"\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = str(form.get(\"csrf_token\", \"\"))\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\", \"\")\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(sent, expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Missing or invalid CSRF token\")\n\n\ndef start_session(request: Request, user_id: int) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user_id\n    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\nclass PasswordChangeRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"A User on a temporary password must set their own first.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef logged_in_user(request: Request):\n    \"\"\"Dependency: the logged-in, active user, or redirect to login.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = users.get_user(request.app.state.database, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if user is None or not user[\"is_active\"]:\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired\n    return user\n\n\ndef current_user(user=Depends(logged_in_user)):\n    \"\"\"Like logged_in_user, but a temporary password blocks everything else.\"\"\"\n    if user[\"must_change_password\"]:\n        raise PasswordChangeRequired\n    return user\n\n\nclass AdminRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised for a logged-in non-Admin on an admin-only URL; becomes a 403 page.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef require_admin(user=Depends(current_user)):\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every admin-only route. Anonymous users redirect first.\"\"\"\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise AdminRequired\n    return user\n---\nname: tdd\ndescription: Test-driven development. Use when the user wants to build features or fix bugs test-first, mentions \"red-green-refactor\", or wants integration tests.\n---\n\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Pages</h1>\n  <p>{% if is_admin %}<a href=\"{{ url_for('new_form') }}\">New page</a> · {% endif %}<a href=\"{{ url_for('admin_home') }}\">Dashboard</a></p>\n  {% if not pages %}<p>No pages yet.</p>{% endif %}\n  <ul>\n  {% for p in pages %}\n    <li>\n      <strong data-status=\"{{ p.status }}\">{{ p.status | capitalize }}</strong>\n      {% if can_edit(user, p) %}<a href=\"{{ url_for('edit_form', page_id=p.id) }}\">{{ p.title }}</a>{% else %}{{ p.title }}{% endif %}\n      <small>{% if p.editor_name %}assigned to {{ p.editor_name }}{% else %}no Assigned editor (Admin only){% endif %}{% if p.is_home %} · Home{% endif %}{% if p.show_in_nav %} · in Navigation (order {{ p.nav_order }}){% endif %}</small>\n    </li>\n  {% endfor %}\n  </ul>\n{% endblock %}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"5d05cd3f-c80f-4b0c-95e1-9ff0fc508e6c","timestamp":"2026-10-06T23:50:59.895Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"16:A small CMS in which the president (Admin) and the other Officers (Editors) write Posts and Pages in a local admin console. Publish run, Deploy, Pending release, Navigation and Slug. \"Published\" means ready for release and \"Live\" means actually on the public site as of the latest Deploy.\n        # proves the session was started.\n        assert response.status_code in (302, 303), (\n            f\"Login as {role} failed with {response.status_code}: expected a redirect\")\n        return c\n\n    return _login\n\"\"\"T07: Pages, Assigned editor and Navigation, tested through the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nimport pytest\nfrom conftest import token_from\n\nfrom app import users\n\nEDITOR_ID = 2\n\n\ndef csrf(c):\n    return token_from(c.get(\"/admin\").text)\n\n\ndef save_page(c, title=\"About\", slug=\"\", body=\"About us.\", action=\"save\",\n              page_id=None, **extra):\n    path = f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}\" if page_id else \"/admin/pages\"\n    data = {\"title\": title, \"slug\": slug, \"body\": body, \"action\": action,\n            \"csrf_token\": csrf(c), **extra}\n    return c.post(path, data=data, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef page_id_of(response):\n    assert response.status_code == 303, response.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/admin/pages/(\\d+)\", response.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef page_post(c, page_id, suffix, **data):\n    return c.post(f\"/admin/pages/{page_id}{suffix}\",\n                  data={\"csrf_token\": csrf(c), **data}, follow_redirects=False)\n\n\ndef row(db_path, page_id, column):\n    return sqlite3.connect(db_path).execute(\n        f\"select {column} from pages where id=?\", (page_id,)).fetchone()[0]\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef other_editor(db_path, make_client):\n    users.create_user(db_path, email=\"other@example.test\", password=\"other-editor-pw\",\n                      role=\"editor\", display_name=\"Other Editor\")\n    c = make_client()\n    c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"other@example.test\", \"password\": \"other-editor-pw\",\n                           \"csrf_token\": token_from(c.get(\"/login\").text)})\n    return c\n\n\n# --- creating and permissions ----------------------------------------------\n1:\"\"\"The `cms` command: serve, publish, deploy.\n5:    uv run cms deploy     # push site/ to the gh-pages branch (Pages serves it)\n15:from app import deploy, settings, users\n19:def main(argv: list[str] | None = None, *, push=deploy.push_site,\n28:    deploy_cmd = sub.add_parser(\"deploy\", help=\"push site/ to gh-pages\")\n29:    deploy_cmd.add_argument(\"--message\", default=\"Publish site\")\n99:    if args.command == \"deploy\":\n103:        bad = deploy.root_absolute_files(settings.SITE)\n105:            print(\"Refusing to deploy: root-absolute paths (href=\\\"/...\\\") in \"\n110:            print(\"Not deployed. Nothing was pushed.\")\n\"\"\"Sessions, CSRF protection and the login requirement for admin routes.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport secrets\n\nfrom fastapi import Depends, HTTPException, Request\nfrom itsdangerous import SignatureExpired, TimestampSigner\nfrom starlette.middleware.sessions import SessionMiddleware\n\nfrom app import users\n\nSESSION_SECONDS = 8 * 60 * 60\n# How far in the future a validly signed session may look. A wall clock that\n# steps backwards (WSL2 and VM clock syncs do) would otherwise log everyone out.\nCLOCK_SKEW_SECONDS = 5 * 60\n\n\nclass SkewTolerantSigner(TimestampSigner):\n    \"\"\"itsdangerous rejects a cookie stamped even 1s ahead of the clock; allow a little.\"\"\"\n\n    def unsign(self, signed_value, max_age=None, return_timestamp=False):\n        try:\n            return super().unsign(signed_value, max_age=max_age,\n                                  return_timestamp=return_timestamp)\n        except SignatureExpired as exc:\n            signed = exc.date_signed\n            # Only raised after the signature itself verified.\n            ahead = signed.timestamp() - self.get_timestamp() if signed else 0\n            if not 0 < ahead <= CLOCK_SKEW_SECONDS:\n                raise\n            value = exc.payload\n            return (value, signed) if return_timestamp else value\n\n\nclass SkewTolerantSessionMiddleware(SessionMiddleware):\n    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):\n        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)\n        self.signer = SkewTolerantSigner(str(kwargs.get(\"secret_key\") or args[1]))\n\n\nclass LoginRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised for an anonymous (or Deactivated) visitor; becomes a redirect.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef csrf_token(request: Request) -> str:\n    token = request.session.get(\"csrf\")\n    if not token:\n        token = request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n    return token\n\n\nasync def verify_csrf(request: Request) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every state-changing route: reject a missing/wrong token.\"\"\"\n    form = await request.form()\n    sent = str(form.get(\"csrf_token\", \"\"))\n    expected = request.session.get(\"csrf\", \"\")\n    if not expected or not secrets.compare_digest(sent, expected):\n        raise HTTPException(status_code=403, detail=\"Missing or invalid CSRF token\")\n\n\ndef start_session(request: Request, user_id: int) -> None:\n    request.session.clear()\n    request.session[\"user_id\"] = user_id\n    request.session[\"csrf\"] = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)\n\n\nclass PasswordChangeRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"A User on a temporary password must set their own first.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef logged_in_user(request: Request):\n    \"\"\"Dependency: the logged-in, active user, or redirect to login.\"\"\"\n    user_id = request.session.get(\"user_id\")\n    user = users.get_user(request.app.state.database, user_id) if user_id else None\n    if user is None or not user[\"is_active\"]:\n        request.session.clear()\n        raise LoginRequired\n    return user\n\n\ndef current_user(user=Depends(logged_in_user)):\n    \"\"\"Like logged_in_user, but a temporary password blocks everything else.\"\"\"\n    if user[\"must_change_password\"]:\n        raise PasswordChangeRequired\n    return user\n\n\nclass AdminRequired(Exception):\n    \"\"\"Raised for a logged-in non-Admin on an admin-only URL; becomes a 403 page.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef require_admin(user=Depends(current_user)):\n    \"\"\"Dependency for every admin-only route. Anonymous users redirect first.\"\"\"\n    if user[\"role\"] != \"admin\":\n        raise AdminRequired\n    return user\n---\nname: tdd\ndescription: Test-driven development. Use when the user wants to build features or fix bugs test-first, mentions \"red-green-refactor\", or wants integration tests.\n---\n\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>Pages</h1>\n  <p>{% if is_admin %}<a href=\"{{ url_for('new_form') }}\">New page</a> · {% endif %}<a href=\"{{ url_for('admin_home') }}\">Dashboard</a></p>\n  {% if not pages %}<p>No pages yet.</p>{% endif %}\n  <ul>\n  {% for p in pages %}\n    <li>\n      <strong data-status=\"{{ p.status }}\">{{ p.status | capitalize }}</strong>\n      {% if can_edit(user, p) %}<a href=\"{{ url_for('edit_form', page_id=p.id) }}\">{{ p.title }}</a>{% else %}{{ p.title }}{% endif %}\n      <small>{% if p.editor_name %}assigned to {{ p.editor_name }}{% else %}no Assigned editor (Admin only){% endif %}{% if p.is_home %} · Home{% endif %}{% if p.show_in_nav %} · in Navigation (order {{ p.nav_order }}){% endif %}</small>\n    </li>\n  {% endfor %}\n  </ul>\n{% endblock > tests/test_dashboard.py <<'EOF'\n\"\"\"T08: dashboard and content list, tested through the HTTP app.\"\"\"\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\n\nimport pytest\nfrom conftest import token_from\n\nfrom app import deploy, users\n\n\ndef csrf(c):\n    return token_from(c.get(\"/admin\").text)\n\n\ndef make_post(c, title, action=\"save\"):\n    r = c.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": title, \"body\": \"x\", \"action\": action,\n                                     \"csrf_token\": csrf(c)}, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert r.status_code == 303, r.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/posts/(\\d+)\", r.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef make_page(c, title, action=\"save\", **extra):\n    r = c.post(\"/admin/pages\", data={\"title\": title, \"body\": \"x\", \"action\": action,\n                                     \"csrf_token\": csrf(c), **extra},\n               follow_redirects=False)\n    assert r.status_code == 303, r.text\n    return int(re.search(r\"/pages/(\\d+)\", r.headers[\"location\"]).group(1))\n\n\ndef section(html, name):\n    \"\"\"The HTML of one dashboard section, marked data-section=\"name\".\"\"\"\n    m = re.search(rf'<section data-section=\"{name}\".*?</section>', html, re.S)\n    return m.group(0) if m else \"\"\n\n\ndef set_updated(db_path, table, item_id, stamp):\n    with sqlite3.connect(db_path) as conn:\n        conn.execute(f\"update {table} set updated_at=? where id=?\", (stamp, item_id))\n\n\n@pytest.fixture\ndef other_editor(db_path, make_client):\n    users.create_user(db_path, email=\"other@example.test\", password=\"other-editor-pw\",\n                      role=\"editor\", display_name=\"Other Editor\")\n    c = make_client()\n    c.post(\"/login\", data={\"email\": \"other@example.test\", \"password\": \"other-editor-pw\",\n                           \"csrf_token\": token_from(c.get(\"/login\").text)})\n    return c\n\n\n# --- access ----------------------------------------------------------------\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"path\", [\"/admin\", \"/admin/content\"])\ndef test_anonymous_is_redirected(client, path):\n    r = client.get(path, follow_redirects=False)\n    assert r.status_code in (302, 303) and r.headers[\"location\"].endswith(\"/login\")\n\n\ndef test_pages_are_readable_at_phone_width(client_as):\n    for path in (\"/admin\", \"/admin/content\"):\n        assert 'name=\"viewport\"' in client_as(\"editor\").get(path).text\n\n\n# --- dashboard -------------------------------------------------------------\ndef test_dashboard_order_is_pending_then_drafts_then_recent(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    make_post(c, \"Live-ish post\", action=\"publish\")\n    make_post(c, \"Half written\")\n    html = c.get(\"/admin\").text\n    order = [html.index(f'data-section=\"{s}\"') for s in (\"pending\", \"drafts\", \"recent\")]\n    assert order == sorted(order)\n    assert \"Live-ish post\" in section(html, \"pending\")\n    assert \"Half written\" not in section(html, \"pending\")\n    assert \"Half written\" in section(html, \"drafts\")\n\n\ndef test_never_deployed_published_item_is_pending_but_drafts_are_not(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    make_page(c, \"About\", action=\"publish\")\n    make_page(c, \"Secret\", action=\"save\")\n    pending = section(c.get(\"/admin\").text, \"pending\")\n    assert \"About\" in pending and \"Secret\" not in pending\n\n\ndef test_deploy_clears_pending_and_later_edit_brings_it_back(client_as, db_path):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    pid = make_post(c, \"News item\", action=\"publish\")\n    deploy.record_deploy(db_path)\n    set_updated(db_path, \"posts\", pid, \"2000-01-01T00:00:00+00:00\")\n    assert \"News item\" not in section(c.get(\"/admin\").text, \"pending\")\n    set_updated(db_path, \"posts\", pid, \"2999-01-01T00:00:00+00:00\")\n    assert \"News item\" in section(c.get(\"/admin\").text, \"pending\")\n\n\ndef test_recent_shows_only_my_items(client_as, other_editor):\n    other_editor.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"Theirs\", \"body\": \"x\",\n                      \"csrf_token\": csrf(other_editor)})\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    make_post(c, \"Mine one\")\n    recent = section(c.get(\"/admin\").text, \"recent\")\n    assert \"Mine one\" in recent and \"Theirs\" not in recent\n\n\ndef test_unassigned_pages_section_is_admin_only(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    make_page(admin, \"History\")\n    assert \"History\" in section(admin.get(\"/admin\").text, \"unassigned\")\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert section(editor.get(\"/admin\").text, \"unassigned\") == \"\"\n    assert \"History\" not in editor.get(\"/admin\").text.split(\"data-section=\\\"recent\\\"\")[0]\n\n\n# --- content list ----------------------------------------------------------\ndef test_list_shows_posts_and_pages_with_status_badges(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    make_post(c, \"A post\", action=\"publish\")\n    make_page(c, \"A page\")\n    html = c.get(\"/admin/content\").text\n    assert \"A post\" in html and \"A page\" in html\n    assert 'class=\"badge badge-published\"' in html\n    assert 'class=\"badge badge-draft\"' in html\n\n\ndef test_filters_by_status_type_and_author(client_as, other_editor):\n    other_editor.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"Other draft\", \"body\": \"x\",\n                      \"csrf_token\": csrf(other_editor)})\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    make_post(c, \"Admin published\", action=\"publish\")\n    make_page(c, \"Admin page\")\n    get = lambda q: c.get(\"/admin/content\" + q).text  # noqa: E731\n    assert \"Admin page\" not in get(\"?type=post\")\n    assert \"Admin published\" not in get(\"?type=page\")\n    assert \"Other draft\" not in get(\"?status=published\")\n    assert \"Admin published\" not in get(\"?status=draft\")\n    other_id = users.get_user_by_email(c.app.state.database, \"other@example.test\")[\"id\"]\n    by_author = get(f\"?author={other_id}\")\n    assert \"Other draft\" in by_author and \"Admin published\" not in by_author\n\n\ndef test_mine_shortcut_shows_only_my_items(client_as, other_editor):\n    other_editor.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"Theirs\", \"body\": \"x\",\n                      \"csrf_token\": csrf(other_editor)})\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    make_post(c, \"Mine\")\n    html = c.get(\"/admin/content?mine=1\").text\n    assert \"Mine\" in html and \"Theirs\" not in html\n\n\ndef test_list_has_no_categories_tags_or_bulk_actions(client_as):\n    html = client_as(\"admin\").get(\"/admin/content\").text.lower()\n    for word in (\"categor\", \"tags\", \"bulk\", 'type=\"checkbox\"'):\n        assert word not in html\n\n\ndef test_other_peoples_rows_have_no_edit_link_for_editors(client_as, other_editor):\n    other_editor.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"Theirs\", \"body\": \"x\",\n                      \"csrf_token\": csrf(other_editor)})\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    make_page(admin, \"Unassigned page\")\n    html = client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/content\").text\n    assert \"Theirs\" in html and \"Unassigned page\" in html\n    assert \"/admin/posts/1\" not in html and \"/admin/pages/1\" not in html\n\n\ndef test_own_rows_and_assigned_pages_are_editable_for_editors(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    editor_id = users.get_user_by_email(admin.app.state.database,\n                                        \"editor@example.test\")[\"id\"]\n    pid = make_page(admin, \"Assigned\", assigned_editor_id=str(editor_id))\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = make_post(c, \"Own\")\n    html = c.get(\"/admin/content\").text\n    assert f\"/admin/pages/{pid}\" in html and f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\" in html\n\n\ndef test_items_changed_since_last_deploy_are_marked(client_as, db_path):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    pid = make_post(c, \"Old\", action=\"publish\")\n    deploy.record_deploy(db_path)\n    set_updated(db_path, \"posts\", pid, \"2000-01-01T00:00:00+00:00\")\n    assert \"changed-since-deploy\" not in c.get(\"/admin/content\").text\n    make_post(c, \"Fresh\")\n    assert \"changed-since-deploy\" in c.get(\"/admin/content\").text\nEOF\ngrep -n \"def \" app/users.py | head test_deploy_clears_pending_and_later_edit_brings_it_back(client_as, db_path):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    pid = make_post(c, \"News item\", action=\"publish\")\n    deploy.record_deploy(db_path)\n    set_updated(db_path, \"posts\", pid, \"2000-01-01T00:00:00+00:00\")\n    assert \"News item\" not in section(c.get(\"/admin\").text, \"pending\")\n    set_updated(db_path, \"posts\", pid, \"2999-01-01T00:00:00+00:00\")\n    assert \"News item\" in section(c.get(\"/admin\").text, \"pending\")\n\n\ndef test_recent_shows_only_my_items(client_as, other_editor):\n    other_editor.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"Theirs\", \"body\": \"x\",\n                      \"csrf_token\": csrf(other_editor)})\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    make_post(c, \"Mine one\")\n    recent = section(c.get(\"/admin\").text, \"recent\")\n    assert \"Mine one\" in recent and \"Theirs\" not in recent\n\n\ndef test_unassigned_pages_section_is_admin_only(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    make_page(admin, \"History\")\n    assert \"History\" in section(admin.get(\"/admin\").text, \"unassigned\")\n    editor = client_as(\"editor\")\n    assert section(editor.get(\"/admin\").text, \"unassigned\") == \"\"\n    assert \"History\" not in editor.get(\"/admin\").text.split(\"data-section=\\\"recent\\\"\")[0]\n\n\n# --- content list ----------------------------------------------------------\ndef test_list_shows_posts_and_pages_with_status_badges(client_as):\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    make_post(c, \"A post\", action=\"publish\")\n    make_page(c, \"A page\")\n    html = c.get(\"/admin/content\").text\n    assert \"A post\" in html and \"A page\" in html\n    assert 'class=\"badge badge-published\"' in html\n    assert 'class=\"badge badge-draft\"' in html\n\n\ndef test_filters_by_status_type_and_author(client_as, other_editor):\n    other_editor.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"Other draft\", \"body\": \"x\",\n                      \"csrf_token\": csrf(other_editor)})\n    c = client_as(\"admin\")\n    make_post(c, \"Admin published\", action=\"publish\")\n    make_page(c, \"Admin page\")\n    get = lambda q: c.get(\"/admin/content\" + q).text  # noqa: E731\n    assert \"Admin page\" not in get(\"?type=post\")\n    assert \"Admin published\" not in get(\"?type=page\")\n    assert \"Other draft\" not in get(\"?status=published\")\n    assert \"Admin published\" not in get(\"?status=draft\")\n    other_id = users.get_user_by_email(c.app.state.database, \"other@example.test\")[\"id\"]\n    by_author = get(f\"?author={other_id}\")\n    assert \"Other draft\" in by_author and \"Admin published\" not in by_author\n\n\ndef test_mine_shortcut_shows_only_my_items(client_as, other_editor):\n    other_editor.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"Theirs\", \"body\": \"x\",\n                      \"csrf_token\": csrf(other_editor)})\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    make_post(c, \"Mine\")\n    html = c.get(\"/admin/content?mine=1\").text\n    assert \"Mine\" in html and \"Theirs\" not in html\n\n\ndef test_list_has_no_categories_tags_or_bulk_actions(client_as):\n    html = client_as(\"admin\").get(\"/admin/content\").text.lower()\n    for word in (\"categor\", \"tags\", \"bulk\", 'type=\"checkbox\"'):\n        assert word not in html\n\n\ndef test_other_peoples_rows_have_no_edit_link_for_editors(client_as, other_editor):\n    other_editor.post(\"/admin/posts\", data={\"title\": \"Theirs\", \"body\": \"x\",\n                      \"csrf_token\": csrf(other_editor)})\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    make_page(admin, \"Unassigned page\")\n    html = client_as(\"editor\").get(\"/admin/content\").text\n    assert \"Theirs\" in html and \"Unassigned page\" in html\n    assert \"/admin/posts/1\" not in html and \"/admin/pages/1\" not in html\n\n\ndef test_own_rows_and_assigned_pages_are_editable_for_editors(client_as):\n    admin = client_as(\"admin\")\n    editor_id = users.get_user_by_email(admin.app.state.database,\n                                        \"editor@example.test\")[\"id\"]\n    pid = make_page(admin, \"Assigned\", assigned_editor_id=str(editor_id))\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    post_id = make_post(c, \"Own\")\n    html = c.get(\"/admin/content\").text\n    assert f\"/admin/pages/{pid}\" in html and f\"/admin/posts/{post_id}\" in html\n\n\ndef test_items_changed_since_last_deploy_are_marked(client_as, db_path):\n    c = client_as(\"editor\")\n    pid = make_post(c, \"Old\", action=\"publish\")\n    deploy.record_deploy(db_path)\n    set_updated(db_path, \"posts\", pid, \"2000-01-01T00:00:00+00:00\")\n    assert \"changed-since-deploy\" not in c.get(\"/admin/content\").text\n    make_post(c, \"Fresh\")\n    assert \"changed-since-deploy\" in c.get(\"/admin/content\").text\nEOF\ngrep -n \"def \" app/users.py | head
